## 9) Complete test suite (compile + coverage + retry + idempotency)

### Test strategy (enterprise-grade)
- No real callouts: use `HttpCalloutMock`
- Validate:
  1) RetryPolicy json parsing
  2) Idempotency duplicate protection
  3) Worker happy path (AI_DECISION → POST_RESULT → Succeeded)
  4) Worker retry path (forced exception → RetryScheduled row)
  5) Scheduler enqueues jobs for due retries

#### 9.1 Callout mock
```apex
@IsTest
global class AiCalloutMock implements HttpCalloutMock {
    global HTTPResponse respond(HTTPRequest req) {
        HttpResponse r = new HttpResponse();
        r.setStatusCode(200);
        r.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
        r.setBody('{"decision":"APPROVE","confidence":0.9,"reasons":["ok"]}');
        return r;
    }
}
```

#### 9.2 TestDataFactory (minimal)
```apex
@IsTest
public class KernelTestData {
    public static Orchestration__c makeOrch() {
        Orchestration__c o = new Orchestration__c();
        o.Name__c = 'TestOrch';
        o.Version__c = '1';
        o.Is_Active__c = true;
        o.Default_Retry_Policy__c = '{"maxAttempts":2,"baseDelaySeconds":1,"backoffMultiplier":1,"maxDelaySeconds":5}';
        o.Ai_Provider__c = 'Einstein';
        o.Ai_Named_Credential__c = 'Einstein_NC';
        insert o;
        return o;
    }

    public static Execution__c makeExec(Id orchId) {
        Execution__c e = new Execution__c();
        e.Orchestration__c = orchId;
        e.Status__c = 'Running';
        e.Started_At__c = System.now();
        e.Deadline_At__c = System.now().addMinutes(10);
        e.Current_Step__c = 'AI_DECISION';
        e.Correlation_Id__c = 'corr-1';
        e.Request_Id__c = 'req-1';
        e.Idempotency_Key__c = 'exec-1';
        insert e;
        return e;
    }
}
```

#### 9.3 Tests
```apex
@IsTest
public class KernelSuite_Test {

    @IsTest
    static void testRetryPolicyFromJson() {
        RetryPolicy p = RetryPolicy.fromJson('{"maxAttempts":5,"baseDelaySeconds":2,"backoffMultiplier":2,"maxDelaySeconds":10}');
        System.assertEquals(5, p.maxAttempts);
        System.assertEquals(2, p.computeDelaySeconds(1));
    }

    @IsTest
    static void testIdempotencyDuplicate() {
        Orchestration__c o = KernelTestData.makeOrch();
        Execution__c e = KernelTestData.makeExec(o.Id);

        StepExecution__c a = Idempotency.registerAttempt(e.Id, 'AI_DECISION', 'k1', 1);
        System.assertNotEquals(null, a.Id);

        Boolean threw = false;
        try {
            Idempotency.registerAttempt(e.Id, 'AI_DECISION', 'k1', 1);
        } catch (Idempotency.DuplicateStepException ex) {
            threw = true;
        }
        System.assert(threw, 'Expected DuplicateStepException');
    }

    @IsTest
    static void testWorkerHappyPath() {
        Test.setMock(HttpCalloutMock.class, new AiCalloutMock());

        Orchestration__c o = KernelTestData.makeOrch();
        Execution__c e = KernelTestData.makeExec(o.Id);

        Test.startTest();
        System.enqueueJob(new OrchestratorWorker(e.Id, 'AI_DECISION', 'idem-1', 1));
        Test.stopTest();

        Execution__c e2 = [SELECT Status__c, Result_JSON__c, Current_Step__c FROM Execution__c WHERE Id=:e.Id];
        System.assertEquals('Succeeded', e2.Status__c);
        System.assertNotEquals(null, e2.Result_JSON__c);

        List<StepExecution__c> steps = [
            SELECT Step_Key__c, Status__c
            FROM StepExecution__c
            WHERE Execution__c = :e.Id
        ];
        System.assert(steps.size() >= 2, 'Expect AI_DECISION and POST_RESULT steps');
    }

    @IsTest
    static void testWorkerRetryScheduledOnFailure() {
        // Override provider to throw
        IAiProvider bad = new IAiProvider() {
            public String generateJson(String prompt, Map<String,Object> ctx) { throw new CalloutException('boom'); }
            public String providerName(){ return 'X'; }
            public String modelName(){ return 'Y'; }
            public String promptVersion(){ return 'v1'; }
        };
        AiProviderFactory.setOverride(bad);

        Orchestration__c o = KernelTestData.makeOrch();
        Execution__c e = KernelTestData.makeExec(o.Id);

        Test.startTest();
        System.enqueueJob(new OrchestratorWorker(e.Id, 'AI_DECISION', 'idem-2', 1));
        Test.stopTest();

        List<StepExecution__c> retries = [
            SELECT Status__c
            FROM StepExecution__c
            WHERE Execution__c = :e.Id AND Status__c = 'RetryScheduled'
        ];
        System.assert(!retries.isEmpty(), 'Expected a RetryScheduled row');
    }

    @IsTest
    static void testSchedulerEnqueuesDueRetries() {
        Orchestration__c o = KernelTestData.makeOrch();
        Execution__c e = KernelTestData.makeExec(o.Id);

        // Create a due retry row
        StepExecution__c r = new StepExecution__c(
            Execution__c = e.Id,
            Step_Key__c = 'AI_DECISION',
            Attempt__c = 1,
            Status__c = 'RetryScheduled',
            Next_Run_At__c = System.now().addSeconds(-1)
        );
        insert r;

        Test.setMock(HttpCalloutMock.class, new AiCalloutMock());

        Test.startTest();
        (new OrchestratorRetryScheduler()).execute(null);
        Test.stopTest();

        // After scheduler, row should be pending or succeeded downstream
        StepExecution__c r2 = [SELECT Status__c FROM StepExecution__c WHERE Id=:r.Id];
        System.assertEquals('Pending', r2.Status__c);
    }
}
```

## 10) Your “don’t shrink AI + Tableau Next” requirement — confirmed

What you now have is **an enterprise kernel** foundation with:
- **Retry policy per step**
- **Idempotency enforced via External ID unique key**
- **Max execution duration guard via Deadline_At**
- **AI provider seam (Einstein/Agentforce-ready)**
- **Tableau Next-ready observability schema (Execution + StepExecution)**

Next platform-strength increments (Priority 2) will extend this into:
- fan-out/fan-in DAG execution
- conditional branching
- compensation steps (Saga)

…but we **do not** touch that until this compiles cleanly and tests pass.
